To address these challenges, we introduce fedCI, a federated conditional independence test that rigorously handles heterogeneous datasets with non-identical sets of variables, site-specific effects, and mixed variable types, including continuous, ordinal, binary, and categorical variables. At its core, fedCI uses a federated Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares (IRLS) procedure to estimate the parameters of generalized linear models underlying likelihood-ratio tests for conditional independence. Building on this, we develop fedCI-IOD, a federated extension of the Integration of Overlapping Datasets (IOD) algorithm, that replaces its meta-analysis strategy and enables, for the fist time, federated causal discovery under latent confounding across distributed and heterogeneous datasets. By aggregating evidence federatively, fedCI-IOD not only preserves privacy but also substantially enhances statistical power, achieving performance comparable to fully pooled analyses and mitigating artifacts from low local sample sizes. Our tools are publicly available as the fedCI Python package, a privacy-preserving R implementation of IOD, and a web application for the fedCI-IOD pipeline, providing versatile, user-friendly solutions for federated conditional independence testing and causal discovery.

</details>


### [155] [Balancing Privacy-Quality-Efficiency in Federated Learning through Round-Based Interleaving of Protection Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05158)
*Yenan Wang,Carla Fabiana Chiasserini,Elad Michael Schiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Alt-FL是一个隐私保护的联邦学习框架，结合差分隐私、同态加密和合成数据，通过轮次交错策略实现隐私保护、学习质量和效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中平衡隐私保护、学习质量和效率具有挑战性。现有隐私保护机制如差分隐私会降低学习质量，同态加密则带来显著系统开销。需要一种能灵活权衡质量-效率并提供隐私保护的解决方案。

Method: 提出Alt-FL框架，结合差分隐私、同态加密和合成数据，采用轮次交错策略。引入三种新方法：隐私交错、带差分隐私的合成交错、带同态加密的合成交错。使用LeNet-5模型在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST数据集上评估，对抗代表性重建攻击。

Result: 在研究的攻击模型和数据集上，隐私交错在高隐私保护级别下实现最平衡的权衡，而差分隐私方法在中等隐私要求下更优。引入攻击者中心框架公平比较不同防御方法。

Conclusion: Alt-FL框架通过灵活的交错策略为不同隐私和资源约束下的联邦学习提供了有效的隐私保护解决方案，研究结果为选择隐私保护方法提供了依据。

Abstract: In federated learning (FL), balancing privacy protection, learning quality, and efficiency remains a challenge. Privacy protection mechanisms, such as Differential Privacy (DP), degrade learning quality, or, as in the case of Homomorphic Encryption (HE), incur substantial system overhead. To address this, we propose Alt-FL, a privacy-preserving FL framework that combines DP, HE, and synthetic data via a novel round-based interleaving strategy. Alt-FL introduces three new methods, Privacy Interleaving (PI), Synthetic Interleaving with DP (SI/DP), and Synthetic Interleaving with HE (SI/HE), that enable flexible quality-efficiency trade-offs while providing privacy protection.
  We systematically evaluate Alt-FL against representative reconstruction attacks, including Deep Leakage from Gradients, Inverting Gradients, When the Curious Abandon Honesty, and Robbing the Fed, using a LeNet-5 model on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST. To enable fair comparison between DP- and HE-based defenses, we introduce a new attacker-centric framework that compares empirical attack success rates across the three proposed interleaving methods. Our results show that, for the studied attacker model and dataset, PI achieves the most balanced trade-offs at high privacy protection levels, while DP-based methods are preferable at intermediate privacy requirements. We also discuss how such results can be the basis for selecting privacy-preserving FL methods under varying privacy and resource constraints.

</details>


### [156] [Incentive Aware AI Regulations: A Credal Characterisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05175)
*Anurag Singh,Julian Rodemann,Rajeev Verma,Siu Lun Chau,Krikamol Muandet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将AI监管转化为机制设计问题，通过许可证机制让ML提供商根据模型性能选择市场占有率，实现完美市场结果：非合规提供商自我排除，合规提供商参与市场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的机器学习应用需要严格监管，但ML提供商常规避监管以降低成本。需要设计可执行的监管机制，确保合规提供商参与市场而非合规提供商自我排除。

Method: 将AI监管建模为不确定性下的机制设计问题，提出监管机制框架：将模型经验证据映射到市场占有率许可证。提供商从许可证集合中选择，迫使其对模型满足监管要求的能力下注。

Result: 证明机制具有完美市场结果（非合规提供商自我排除且合规提供商参与）当且仅当非合规分布集合构成信度集（封闭、凸的概率测度集）。建立了监管机制与非合规分布集合之间的对偶性。

Conclusion: 该框架为机制设计与不精确概率的交叉领域提供新见解，为开发可执行的AI监管奠定了基础。通过预测中虚假特征使用和公平性监管的实验验证了机制的实际可行性。

Abstract: While high-stakes ML applications demand strict regulations, strategic ML providers often evade them to lower development costs. To address this challenge, we cast AI regulation as a mechanism design problem under uncertainty and introduce regulation mechanisms: a framework that maps empirical evidence from models to a license for some market share. The providers can select from a set of licenses, effectively forcing them to bet on their model's ability to fulfil regulation. We aim at regulation mechanisms that achieve perfect market outcome, i.e. (a) drive non-compliant providers to self-exclude, and (b) ensure participation from compliant providers. We prove that a mechanism has perfect market outcome if and only if the set of non-compliant distributions forms a credal set, i.e., a closed, convex set of probability measures. This result connects mechanism design and imprecise probability by establishing a duality between regulation mechanisms and the set of non-compliant distributions. We also demonstrate these mechanisms in practice via experiments on regulating use of spurious features for prediction and fairness. Our framework provides new insights at the intersection of mechanism design and imprecise probability, offering a foundation for development of enforceable AI regulations.

</details>


### [157] [Towards a data-scale independent regulariser for robust sparse identification of non-linear dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05201)
*Jay Raut,Daniel N. Wilke,Stephan Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STCV算法通过使用无量纲统计指标替代传统基于幅度的阈值处理，解决了数据归一化对稀疏回归方法（特别是SINDy框架）的负面影响，使系统辨识对任意数据缩放具有不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据归一化作为常见的预处理步骤，会严重扭曲基于幅度的稀疏回归方法对控制方程的发现，特别是在SINDy框架中。归一化与测量噪声的相互作用会破坏稀疏性假设，导致发现密集、不可解释且物理不正确的模型。

Method: 提出序列变异系数阈值法（STCV），这是一种计算高效的稀疏回归算法。STCV用无量纲统计指标"系数存在性"（CP）替代传统的基于幅度的阈值处理，CP评估模型库中候选项的统计有效性和一致性。这种从幅度到统计显著性的转变使发现过程对任意数据缩放具有不变性。

Result: 在规范动力系统和实际工程问题（包括物理质量-弹簧-阻尼器实验）上的综合基准测试表明，STCV在归一化、噪声数据集上始终显著优于标准STLSQ和E-SINDy。STCV方法即使在其他方法失败时也能成功识别正确的稀疏物理定律。

Conclusion: STCV通过减轻归一化的扭曲效应，使稀疏系统辨识成为更可靠和自动化的现实应用工具，从而增强了模型的可解释性和可信度。

Abstract: Data normalisation, a common and often necessary preprocessing step in engineering and scientific applications, can severely distort the discovery of governing equations by magnitudebased sparse regression methods. This issue is particularly acute for the Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) framework, where the core assumption of sparsity is undermined by the interaction between data scaling and measurement noise. The resulting discovered models can be dense, uninterpretable, and physically incorrect. To address this critical vulnerability, we introduce the Sequential Thresholding of Coefficient of Variation (STCV), a novel, computationally efficient sparse regression algorithm that is inherently robust to data scaling. STCV replaces conventional magnitude-based thresholding with a dimensionless statistical metric, the Coefficient Presence (CP), which assesses the statistical validity and consistency of candidate terms in the model library. This shift from magnitude to statistical significance makes the discovery process invariant to arbitrary data scaling. Through comprehensive benchmarking on canonical dynamical systems and practical engineering problems, including a physical mass-spring-damper experiment, we demonstrate that STCV consistently and significantly outperforms standard Sequential Thresholding Least Squares (STLSQ) and Ensemble-SINDy (E-SINDy) on normalised, noisy datasets. The results show that STCV-based methods can successfully identify the correct, sparse physical laws even when other methods fail. By mitigating the distorting effects of normalisation, STCV makes sparse system identification a more reliable and automated tool for real-world applications, thereby enhancing model interpretability and trustworthiness.

</details>


### [158] [Stable-LoRA: Stabilizing Feature Learning of Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05204)
*Yize Wu,Ke Gao,Ling Li,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Stable-LoRA：一种通过权重收缩优化策略解决LoRA特征学习不稳定性的方法，在保持非零初始化优势的同时提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA作为一种广泛采用的参数高效微调方法，虽然经验上有效，但其理论基础特别是特征学习稳定性方面理解不足。研究发现LoRA在适当超参数和初始化下可以实现自稳定特征学习，但必要的非零初始化会破坏这种自稳定性，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出Stable-LoRA，一种权重收缩优化策略，通过在训练早期阶段逐步收缩A矩阵来动态增强LoRA特征学习的稳定性。该方法保留了非零初始化的优势，同时有效消除不稳定性。

Result: 实验表明Stable-LoRA在各种模型和任务中持续优于其他基线方法，且无需额外内存使用，计算开销可忽略不计。理论分析和实证验证都表明该方法能有效消除LoRA特征学习的不稳定性。

Conclusion: Stable-LoRA通过权重收缩策略解决了LoRA特征学习的不稳定性问题，在保持参数高效微调优势的同时提升了性能，为LoRA的理论理解和实际应用提供了重要改进。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely adopted parameter-efficient method for fine-tuning Large Langauge Models. It updates the weight matrix as $W=W_0+sBA$, where $W_0$ is the original frozen weight, $s$ is a scaling factor and $A$,$B$ are trainable low-rank matrices. Despite its robust empirical effectiveness, the theoretical foundations of LoRA remain insufficiently understood, particularly with respect to feature learning stability. In this paper, we first establish that, LoRA can, in principle, naturally achieve and sustain stable feature learning (i.e., be self-stabilized) under appropriate hyper-parameters and initializations of $A$ and $B$. However, we also uncover a fundamental limitation that the necessary non-zero initialization of $A$ compromises self-stability, leading to suboptimal performances. To address this challenge, we propose Stable-LoRA, a weight-shrinkage optimization strategy that dynamically enhances stability of LoRA feature learning. By progressively shrinking $A$ during the earliest training steps, Stable-LoRA is both theoretically and empirically validated to effectively eliminate instability of LoRA feature learning while preserving the benefits of the non-zero start. Experiments show that Stable-LoRA consistently outperforms other baselines across diverse models and tasks, with no additional memory usage and only negligible computation overheads. The code is available at https://github.com/Yize-Wu/Stable-LoRA.

</details>


### [159] [Early Warning of Intraoperative Adverse Events via Transformer-Driven Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05212)
*Xueyao Wang,Xiuding Cai,Honglin Shang,Yaoyao Zhu,Yu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了IAENet，一个基于Transformer的多标签学习框架，用于术中不良事件的早期预警，通过改进的TAFiLM模块融合静态和动态临床数据，并引入LCRLoss解决类别不平衡和事件间依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中不良事件早期预警对降低手术风险和改善患者安全至关重要。现有深度学习方法存在三个主要问题：忽视不良事件间的依赖关系、未充分利用异质临床数据、以及医疗数据集中固有的类别不平衡问题。

Method: 1. 构建首个多标签不良事件数据集(MuAE)，涵盖六种关键事件；2. 提出基于Transformer的多标签学习框架IAENet，包含改进的TAFiLM模块用于静态协变量和动态变量的鲁棒融合及复杂时间依赖建模；3. 引入带共现正则化的标签约束重加权损失(LCRLoss)，有效缓解事件内不平衡并强制频繁共现事件间的结构化一致性。

Result: IAENet在5、10和15分钟早期预警任务上持续优于强基线，平均F1分数分别提升+5.05%、+2.82%和+7.57%。这些结果突显了IAENet在支持临床实践中智能术中决策的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了术中不良事件预测中的关键挑战，通过创新的多标签学习框架IAENet，有效融合异质临床数据、建模事件间依赖关系并处理类别不平衡，为临床智能决策支持提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Early warning of intraoperative adverse events plays a vital role in reducing surgical risk and improving patient safety. While deep learning has shown promise in predicting the single adverse event, several key challenges remain: overlooking adverse event dependencies, underutilizing heterogeneous clinical data, and suffering from the class imbalance inherent in medical datasets. To address these issues, we construct the first Multi-label Adverse Events dataset (MuAE) for intraoperative adverse events prediction, covering six critical events. Next, we propose a novel Transformerbased multi-label learning framework (IAENet) that combines an improved Time-Aware Feature-wise Linear Modulation (TAFiLM) module for static covariates and dynamic variables robust fusion and complex temporal dependencies modeling. Furthermore, we introduce a Label-Constrained Reweighting Loss (LCRLoss) with co-occurrence regularization to effectively mitigate intra-event imbalance and enforce structured consistency among frequently co-occurring events. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IAENet consistently outperforms strong baselines on 5, 10, and 15-minute early warning tasks, achieving improvements of +5.05%, +2.82%, and +7.57% on average F1 score. These results highlight the potential of IAENet for supporting intelligent intraoperative decision-making in clinical practice.

</details>


### [160] [The Geometric Inductive Bias of Grokking: Bypassing Phase Transitions via Architectural Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05228)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过干预性架构修改研究Transformer中的grokking现象，发现限制表示幅度和消除数据依赖注意力路由可以显著加速或消除延迟泛化，但效果依赖于任务对称性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机制可解释性方法主要依赖训练后分析，本文采用干预性方法，通过先验修改架构拓扑来观察训练动态，研究Transformer在循环模加法任务中grokking现象是否由特定架构自由度延长记忆阶段导致。

Method: 1. 引入完全有界的球形拓扑：在残差流中强制L2归一化，使用固定温度尺度的解嵌入矩阵，消除幅度自由度；2. 均匀注意力消融：用均匀分布替代数据依赖的查询-键路由，将注意力层简化为连续词袋聚合器；3. 使用非交换S5置换组合作为负对照，验证加速效果是否特定于任务几何对齐。

Result: 1. 球形拓扑在没有权重衰减的情况下将grokking起始时间减少20倍以上；2. 均匀注意力消融模型在所有种子中实现100%泛化，完全绕过grokking延迟；3. 在S5任务上强制球形约束不会加速泛化，表明消除记忆阶段强烈依赖于架构先验与任务内在对称性的对齐。

Conclusion: 架构自由度显著影响grokking现象，提供干预性证据表明架构先验与任务对称性的对齐对训练动态具有预测性结构视角，为理解延迟泛化机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability typically relies on post-hoc analysis of trained networks. We instead adopt an interventional approach: testing hypotheses a priori by modifying architectural topology to observe training dynamics. We study grokking - delayed generalization in Transformers trained on cyclic modular addition (Zp) - investigating if specific architectural degrees of freedom prolong the memorization phase.
  We identify two independent structural factors in standard Transformers: unbounded representational magnitude and data-dependent attention routing. First, we introduce a fully bounded spherical topology enforcing L2 normalization throughout the residual stream and an unembedding matrix with a fixed temperature scale. This removes magnitude-based degrees of freedom, reducing grokking onset time by over 20x without weight decay. Second, a Uniform Attention Ablation overrides data-dependent query-key routing with a uniform distribution, reducing the attention layer to a Continuous Bag-of-Words (CBOW) aggregator. Despite removing adaptive routing, these models achieve 100% generalization across all seeds and bypass the grokking delay entirely.
  To evaluate whether this acceleration is a task-specific geometric alignment rather than a generic optimization stabilizer, we use non-commutative S5 permutation composition as a negative control. Enforcing spherical constraints on S5 does not accelerate generalization. This suggests eliminating the memorization phase depends strongly on aligning architectural priors with the task's intrinsic symmetries. Together, these findings provide interventional evidence that architectural degrees of freedom substantially influence grokking, suggesting a predictive structural perspective on training dynamics.

</details>


### [161] [SlideSparse: Fast and Flexible (2N-2):2N Structured Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05232)
*Hanyong Shao,Yingbo Hao,Ting Song,Yan Xia,Di Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Xun Wu,Songchen Xu,Le Xu,Li Dong,Zewen Chi,Yi Zou,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlideSparse系统首次在商用GPU上为(2N-2):2N稀疏模式（如6:8）提供稀疏张量核心加速，通过滑动窗口分解和激活提升技术实现精度保持的LLM推理加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA的2:4稀疏张量核心虽然提供2倍吞吐量，但要求严格的50%剪枝，这会严重损害LLM推理精度。而更温和的(2N-2):2N模式（如6:8，25%剪枝）能保持精度，但缺乏硬件支持，只能回退到密集执行而无法利用稀疏性优势

Method: SlideSparse采用滑动窗口分解技术，将任意(2N-2):2N权重块重构为N-1个重叠的2:4兼容窗口，保持精度无损；激活提升技术将相应的激活重排融合到每令牌量化中，成本极低。系统集成到vLLM框架中

Result: 在计算受限工作负载上，实测加速比达到1.33倍，接近理论上限N/(N-1)=4/3（对于6:8稀疏度）。在Qwen2.5-7B等模型上验证了(2N-2):2N作为精度保持的LLM加速实用路径

Conclusion: SlideSparse首次实现了商用GPU上对(2N-2):2N稀疏模式的稀疏张量核心加速，为保持精度的LLM推理加速提供了实用解决方案，填补了硬件支持空白

Abstract: NVIDIA's 2:4 Sparse Tensor Cores deliver 2x throughput but demand strict 50% pruning -- a ratio that collapses LLM reasoning accuracy (Qwen3: 54% to 15%). Milder $(2N-2):2N$ patterns (e.g., 6:8, 25% pruning) preserve accuracy yet receive no hardware support, falling back to dense execution without any benefit from sparsity. We present SlideSparse, the first system to unlock Sparse Tensor Core acceleration for the $(2N-2):2N$ model family on commodity GPUs. Our Sliding Window Decomposition reconstructs any $(2N-2):2N$ weight block into $N-1$ overlapping 2:4-compliant windows without any accuracy loss; Activation Lifting fuses the corresponding activation rearrangement into per-token quantization at near-zero cost. Integrated into vLLM, SlideSparse is evaluated across various GPUs (A100, H100, B200, RTX 4090, RTX 5080, DGX-spark), precisions (FP4, INT8, FP8, BF16, FP16), and model families (Llama, Qwen, BitNet). On compute-bound workloads, the measured speedup ratio (1.33x) approaches the theoretical upper-bound $N/(N-1)=4/3$ at 6:8 weight sparsity in Qwen2.5-7B, establishing $(2N-2):2N$ as a practical path to accuracy-preserving LLM acceleration. Code available at https://github.com/bcacdwk/vllmbench.

</details>


### [162] [Recursive Inference Machines for Neural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05234)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIMs（递归推理机）是一种将经典推理引擎的递归推理机制显式融入神经推理的框架，能够提升TRMs等神经推理器的性能，在多个推理基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRMs等神经推理器通过结合神经主干和专门推理方案解决复杂问题，而传统随机推理系统使用推理规则从随机模型推导答案。本研究旨在桥接这两种范式，将经典推理引擎的递归推理机制显式融入神经推理框架。

Method: 提出Recursive Inference Machines（RIMs）框架，该框架显式整合了受经典推理引擎启发的递归推理机制。将TRMs表达为RIMs的一个实例，并通过重新加权组件扩展它们。

Result: RIMs在ARC-AGI-1、ARC-AGI-2和Sudoku Extreme等挑战性推理基准测试中取得了更好的性能。此外，RIMs还能改进其他任务的推理，如在表格数据分类任务中超越了TabPFNs。

Conclusion: RIMs成功桥接了神经推理器和传统推理系统，通过显式整合递归推理机制显著提升了推理性能，为神经推理框架的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Neural reasoners such as Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) solve complex problems by combining neural backbones with specialized inference schemes. Such inference schemes have been a central component of stochastic reasoning systems, where inference rules are applied to a stochastic model to derive answers to complex queries. In this work, we bridge these two paradigms by introducing Recursive Inference Machines (RIMs), a neural reasoning framework that explicitly incorporates recursive inference mechanisms inspired by classical inference engines. We show that TRMs can be expressed as an instance of RIMs, allowing us to extend them through a reweighting component, yielding better performance on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including ARC-AGI-1, ARC-AGI-2, and Sudoku Extreme. Furthermore, we show that RIMs can be used to improve reasoning on other tasks, such as the classification of tabular data, outperforming TabPFNs.

</details>


### [163] [A Behaviour-Aware Federated Forecasting Framework for Distributed Stand-Alone Wind Turbines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05263)
*Bowen Li,Xiufeng Liu,Maria Sinziiana Astefanoaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段联邦学习框架：首先基于长期行为统计使用DRS初始化和递归自动分割细化对风力涡轮机进行聚类，然后通过FedAvg训练聚类特定的LSTM模型，用于短期风电功率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的短期风电功率预测对电网调度和市场运营至关重要，但集中化涡轮机数据会引发隐私、成本和异构性问题。需要一种保护隐私且能处理分布式异构涡轮机群的方法。

Method: 两阶段联邦学习框架：1) 使用双轮盘选择(DRS)初始化和递归自动分割细化，基于长期行为统计对涡轮机进行聚类；2) 通过联邦平均(FedAvg)为每个聚类训练特定的LSTM模型进行预测。

Result: 在丹麦400个独立涡轮机上的实验表明，DRS-auto能够发现行为一致的聚类组，在保持数据本地化的同时实现有竞争力的预测精度。行为感知分组始终优于地理分区，并与强大的k-means++基线方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构分布式涡轮机群提供了一种实用的隐私友好解决方案，通过行为感知聚类和联邦学习在保护数据隐私的同时实现了准确的短期风电功率预测。

Abstract: Accurate short-term wind power forecasting is essential for grid dispatch and market operations, yet centralising turbine data raises privacy, cost, and heterogeneity concerns. We propose a two-stage federated learning framework that first clusters turbines by long-term behavioural statistics using Double Roulette Selection (DRS) initialisation with recursive Auto-split refinement, and then trains cluster-specific LSTM models via FedAvg. Experiments on 400 stand-alone turbines in Denmark show that DRS-auto discovers behaviourally coherent groups and achieves competitive forecasting accuracy while preserving data locality. Behaviour-aware grouping consistently outperforms geographic partitioning and matches strong k-means++ baselines, suggesting a practical privacy-friendly solution for heterogeneous distributed turbine fleets.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Word Error Rate: Auditing the Diversity Tax in Speech Recognition through Dataset Cartography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05267)
*Ting-Hui Cheng,Line H. Clemmensen,Sneha Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了ASR系统评估中仅依赖词错误率(WER)的局限性，提出了样本难度指数(SDI)来量化人口统计和声学因素对模型失败的影响，并展示了语义指标如何揭示WER忽略的系统性偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统主要使用词错误率(WER)进行评估，但原始词级指标无法捕捉语义保真度，并且经常掩盖"多样性税"——由于系统性识别失败而对边缘化和非典型说话者造成的过度负担。需要更全面的评估框架来揭示隐藏的系统性偏见。

Method: 1. 系统评估更广泛的非线性和语义指标；2. 引入样本难度指数(SDI)，量化内在人口统计和声学因素如何驱动模型失败；3. 通过数据制图映射SDI；4. 使用EmbER和SemDist等指标暴露WER忽略的隐藏系统性偏见和模型间分歧。

Result: 研究发现EmbER和SemDist等语义指标能够揭示WER忽略的隐藏系统性偏见和模型间分歧。SDI能够有效量化人口统计和声学因素对ASR模型性能的影响，为模型审计提供了新工具。

Conclusion: 该研究是构建稳健审计框架的第一步，使开发者能够在部署前审计和缓解ASR差异。提出的方法为前瞻性安全分析提供了基础，有助于识别和解决ASR系统中的公平性问题。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems are predominantly evaluated using the Word Error Rate (WER). However, raw token-level metrics fail to capture semantic fidelity and routinely obscures the `diversity tax', the disproportionate burden on marginalized and atypical speaker due to systematic recognition failures. In this paper, we explore the limitations of relying solely on lexical counts by systematically evaluating a broader class of non-linear and semantic metrics. To enable rigorous model auditing, we introduce the sample difficulty index (SDI), a novel metric that quantifies how intrinsic demographic and acoustic factors drive model failure. By mapping SDI on data cartography, we demonstrate that metrics EmbER and SemDist expose hidden systemic biases and inter-model disagreements that WER ignores. Finally, our findings are the first steps towards a robust audit framework for prospective safety analysis, empowering developers to audit and mitigate ASR disparities prior to deployment.

</details>


### [165] [Whispering to a Blackbox: Bootstrapping Frozen OCR with Visual Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05276)
*Samandar Samandarov,Nazirjon Ismoiljonov,Abdullah Sattorov,Temirlan Sabyrbayev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Whisperer框架通过扩散模型在像素空间预处理输入，以行为克隆方式提升冻结下游模型性能，在文本识别任务上实现8%绝对错误率降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结预训练模型虽然稳定高效，但在特定任务上因数据分布不匹配而表现不佳。需要一种不修改模型权重就能提升性能的方法。

Method: 提出Whisperer视觉提示框架：1) 使用扩散模型在像素空间预处理输入；2) 通过四阶段训练课程，采用行为克隆方法学习改进策略；3) 随机采样扩散中间输出，选择偶然改进CER的结果，训练模型复制这些成功案例。

Result: 在30万张退化合成文本图像数据集上，实现了8%绝对（10.6%相对）字符错误率降低，超越了CLAHE等手工设计基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过"低语"方式在输入层面增强冻结模型，无需修改权重即可显著提升性能。该方法样本高效，避免了传统强化学习的缺陷，为冻结模型适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: In the landscape of modern machine learning, frozen pre-trained models provide stability and efficiency but often underperform on specific tasks due to mismatched data distributions. This paper introduces the Whisperer, a novel visual prompting framework that learns diffusion-based preprocessors to adapt inputs in pixel space, effectively "whispering" enhancements to frozen downstream models like EasyOCR. By framing the process as behavioral cloning of stochastically discovered improvement policies, our method achieves an 8% absolute (10.6% relative) reduction in Character Error Rate (CER) on a challenging dataset of 300k degraded synthetic text images, surpassing hand-engineered baselines such as CLAHE. The key innovation is a four-stage training curriculum that uses behavioral cloning to amplify "lucky" improvements discovered through the stochastic exploration of a partially trained diffusion model. This approach is highly sample-efficient and avoids the pitfalls of traditional reinforcement learning. Crucially, we frame this not as naive reinforcement learning, but as behavioral cloning of an exploration policy: we stochastically sample intermediate diffusion outputs, select those that improve CER by chance, and then train the model to reproduce them. This bootstrapping curriculum (4 stages over 60 GPU-hours) amplifies random successes into a systematic strategy. In summary, by whispering to the frozen OCR through its inputs, we improve an imperfect classifier without touching its weights.

</details>


### [166] [Knowledge Divergence and the Value of Debate for Scalable Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05293)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了辩论与RLAIF之间的首个形式化联系，通过知识分歧的几何参数化证明了辩论优势的闭式解，并识别了三种知识分歧机制及其对辩论有效性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全领域提出了辩论和基于AI反馈的强化学习（RLAIF）两种可扩展监督方法，但缺乏形式化框架来关联这两种方法并明确辩论何时具有优势。本文旨在填补这一空白，为理解对抗性监督协议何时合理提供理论基础。

Method: 通过模型表示子空间之间的主角度参数化知识分歧的几何结构，推导出辩论优势的精确闭式解。分析三种知识分歧机制（共享、单边、组合），并提供存在性结果和负面结果，展示辩论可实现单个模型无法达到的结果，同时识别协调失败的临界阈值。

Result: 当模型共享相同训练语料时，辩论退化为类似RLAIF的单智能体方法；当模型具有分歧知识时，辩论优势呈现从二次机制（辩论益处可忽略）到线性机制（辩论至关重要）的相变。组合机制中存在协调失败的尖锐阈值，区分有效与无效辩论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了辩论与RLAIF之间的首个形式化联系，为理解对抗性监督协议何时合理提供了几何基础，并连接到具有互补信息模型间潜在知识激发问题。研究结果表明辩论在特定知识分歧机制下具有独特优势，但也存在协调失败的风险。

Abstract: AI safety via debate and reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) are both proposed methods for scalable oversight of advanced AI systems, yet no formal framework relates them or characterizes when debate offers an advantage. We analyze this by parameterizing debate's value through the geometry of knowledge divergence between debating models. Using principal angles between models' representation subspaces, we prove that the debate advantage admits an exact closed form. When models share identical training corpora, debate reduces to RLAIF-like where a single-agent method recovers the same optimum. When models possess divergent knowledge, debate advantage scales with a phase transition from quadratic regime (debate offers negligible benefit) to linear regime (debate is essential). We classify three regimes of knowledge divergence (shared, one-sided, and compositional) and provide existence results showing that debate can achieve outcomes inaccessible to either model alone, alongside a negative result showing that sufficiently strong adversarial incentives cause coordination failure in the compositional regime, with a sharp threshold separating effective from ineffective debate. We offer the first formal connection between debate and RLAIF, a geometric foundation for understanding when adversarial oversight protocols are justified, and connection to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge across models with complementary information.

</details>


### [167] [WavSLM: Single-Stream Speech Language Modeling via WavLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05299)
*Luca Della Libera,Cem Subakan,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WavSLM：一种基于自监督WavLM表征的单码本语音语言模型，通过自回归下一块预测实现语义和声学信息的联合建模，无需文本监督或文本预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将文本领域成功的单流生成预训练范式扩展到语音领域面临挑战，因为语音中语义和声学信息相互纠缠。现有语音语言模型大多依赖文本监督、分层token流或复杂混合架构，偏离了文本领域证明有效的简单自回归训练范式。

Method: 通过量化和蒸馏自监督WavLM表征到单一码本，优化自回归下一块预测目标。该方法在单一token流中联合建模语义和声学信息，无需文本监督或文本预训练。

Result: 在一致性基准测试和语音生成任务中取得竞争性性能，同时使用更少的参数、更少的训练数据，并支持流式推理。

Conclusion: WavSLM展示了简单自回归训练范式可以扩展到语音领域，在单一token流中有效联合建模语义和声学信息，为语音语言模型提供了一种更简洁有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models show that simple autoregressive training can yield scalable and coherent generation, but extending this paradigm to speech remains challenging due to the entanglement of semantic and acoustic information. Most existing speech language models rely on text supervision, hierarchical token streams, or complex hybrid architectures, departing from the single-stream generative pretraining paradigm that has proven effective in text. In this work, we introduce WavSLM, a speech language model trained by quantizing and distilling self-supervised WavLM representations into a single codebook and optimizing an autoregressive next-chunk prediction objective. WavSLM jointly models semantic and acoustic information within a single token stream without text supervision or text pretraining. Despite its simplicity, it achieves competitive performance on consistency benchmarks and speech generation while using fewer parameters, less training data, and supporting streaming inference. Demo samples are available at https://lucadellalib.github.io/wavslm-web/.

</details>


### [168] [GALACTIC: Global and Local Agnostic Counterfactuals for Time-series Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05318)
*Christos Fragkathoulas,Eleni Psaroudaki,Themis Palpanas,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GALACTIC：首个统一框架，通过反事实解释实现无监督时间序列聚类的局部和全局可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列聚类可解释性方法（基于特征归因或元数据）无法识别使实例跨越聚类边界的转变，而反事实解释（CEs）主要局限于监督学习设置，缺乏无监督聚类的统一解释框架

Method: 提出GALACTIC框架：局部层面，通过尊重目标和底层聚类分配的聚类感知优化目标生成扰动；全局层面，制定代表性CE选择问题，采用最小描述长度（MDL）目标提取非冗余的全局解释摘要，证明MDL目标具有超模性，可转化为单调子模集函数，实现高效贪心选择算法

Result: 在UCR Archive上的广泛实验表明，GALACTIC比现有基线方法产生显著更稀疏的局部CEs和更简洁的全局摘要，提供首个通过反事实解释聚类时间序列的统一方法

Conclusion: GALACTIC是首个统一局部和全局反事实可解释性的框架，为无监督时间序列聚类提供有效的解释工具，通过理论保证和实验验证了其优越性

Abstract: Time-series clustering is a fundamental tool for pattern discovery, yet existing explainability methods, primarily based on feature attribution or metadata, fail to identify the transitions that move an instance across cluster boundaries. While Counterfactual Explanations (CEs) identify the minimal temporal perturbations required to alter the prediction of a model, they have been mostly confined to supervised settings. This paper introduces GALACTIC, the first unified framework to bridge local and global counterfactual explainability for unsupervised time-series clustering. At instance level (local), GALACTIC generates perturbations via a cluster-aware optimization objective that respects the target and underlying cluster assignments. At cluster level (global), to mitigate cognitive load and enhance interpretability, we formulate a representative CE selection problem. We propose a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective to extract a non-redundant summary of global explanations that characterize the transitions between clusters. We prove that our MDL objective is supermodular, which allows the corresponding MDL reduction to be framed as a monotone submodular set function. This enables an efficient greedy selection algorithm with provable $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantees. Extensive experimental evaluation on the UCR Archive demonstrates that GALACTIC produces significantly sparser local CEs and more concise global summaries than state-of-the-art baselines adapted for our problem, offering the first unified approach for interpreting clustered time-series through counterfactuals.

</details>


### [169] [FairFinGAN: Fairness-aware Synthetic Financial Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05327)
*Tai Le Quy,Dung Nguyen Tuan,Trung Nguyen Thanh,Duy Tran Cong,Huyen Giang Thi Thu,Frank Hopfgartner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairFinGAN：基于WGAN的框架，用于生成公平的合成金融数据，通过分类器将公平约束直接融入训练过程，在保持数据效用的同时减少偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融数据集常存在偏见，可能导致自动化系统做出不公平决策。需要一种既能生成合成金融数据又能减轻偏见的方法，以支持公平的预测任务。

Method: 提出FairFinGAN，一个基于Wasserstein GAN的框架。通过分类器将公平约束直接融入训练过程，确保合成数据既公平又保持下游预测任务的效用。

Result: 在五个真实金融数据集上评估，与现有GAN数据生成方法比较。实验结果显示，该方法在公平性指标上表现优越，且未显著损失数据效用。

Conclusion: FairFinGAN展示了作为金融应用中偏见感知数据生成工具的潜力，能够在保持数据效用的同时实现更好的公平性。

Abstract: Financial datasets often suffer from bias that can lead to unfair decision-making in automated systems. In this work, we propose FairFinGAN, a WGAN-based framework designed to generate synthetic financial data while mitigating bias with respect to the protected attribute. Our approach incorporates fairness constraints directly into the training process through a classifier, ensuring that the synthetic data is both fair and preserves utility for downstream predictive tasks. We evaluate our proposed model on five real-world financial datasets and compare it with existing GAN-based data generation methods. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior fairness metrics without significant loss in data utility, demonstrating its potential as a tool for bias-aware data generation in financial applications.

</details>


### [170] [InfoFlow KV: Information-Flow-Aware KV Recomputation for Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05353)
*Xin Teng,Canyu Zhang,Shaoyi Zheng,Danyang Zhuo,Tianyi Zhou,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于信息流的KV缓存选择性重计算方法，通过注意力范数信号识别语义相关且结构上能传播信息的关键token，在保持效率的同时提升长上下文问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文问答中的检索增强生成受限于对大量检索内容进行推理时预填充的开销。现有方法依赖启发式规则或表示差异，未能建模所选token是否有效影响生成过程。

Method: 将选择性KV重计算视为信息流问题，使用查询的注意力范数信号识别语义相关且结构上能传播信息的关键token；重构检索块的位置分配，引入信息流引导的块重排序策略。

Result: 在LLM和VLM基准测试中，在可比效率预算下相比先前方法获得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于信息流的方法能更有效地识别对生成有实际影响的token，通过注意力范数信号和推理一致的RoPE几何结构实现更优的KV缓存选择性重计算。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for long-context question answering is bottlenecked by inference-time prefilling over large retrieved contexts. A common strategy is to precompute key-value (KV) caches for individual documents and selectively recompute a small subset of tokens to restore global causal dependencies, but existing methods rely on heuristics or representation discrepancies without modeling whether selected tokens can effectively influence generation. We cast selective KV recomputation as an information flow problem and show that a simple attention-norm signal from the query reliably identifies tokens that are both semantically relevant and structurally positioned to propagate information, when computed under an inference-consistent RoPE geometry. We therefore reconstruct global positional assignments for retrieved chunks and introduce an information-flow-guided chunk reordering strategy. Experiments on LLM and VLM benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over prior methods under comparable efficiency budgets.

</details>


### [171] [Embedded Inter-Subject Variability in Adversarial Learning for Inertial Sensor-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05371)
*Francisco M. Calatrava-Nicolás,Shoko Miyauchi,Vitor Fortes Rey,Paul Lukowicz,Todor Stoyanov,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的深度对抗框架，通过整合主体间变异性的概念来增强人类活动识别的泛化能力，在LOSO交叉验证中优于先前方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人类活动识别中由于主体间变异性导致的模型泛化问题，即不同个体执行相同活动的方式存在差异，影响模型对新个体的识别性能

Method: 提出深度对抗框架，将主体间变异性整合到对抗任务中，鼓励学习主体不变的特征表示，从而提升HAR分类性能

Result: 在三个成熟的HAR数据集上使用留一主体交叉验证，性能优于先前方法；对抗任务有效减少了特征空间中不同用户的主体间变异性，且优于先前工作中的对抗任务

Conclusion: 提出的对抗框架通过整合主体间变异性概念，成功提升了HAR模型的泛化能力，为处理主体间差异问题提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using data from wearable inertial sensors. An important challenge in HAR is the model's generalization capabilities to new unseen individuals due to inter-subject variability, i.e., the same activity is performed differently by different individuals. To address this problem, we propose a novel deep adversarial framework that integrates the concept of inter-subject variability in the adversarial task, thereby encouraging subject-invariant feature representations and enhancing the classification performance in the HAR problem. Our approach outperforms previous methods in three well-established HAR datasets using a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation. Further results indicate that our proposed adversarial task effectively reduces inter-subject variability among different users in the feature space, and it outperforms adversarial tasks from previous works when integrated into our framework. Code: https://github.com/FranciscoCalatrava/EmbeddedSubjectVariability.git

</details>


### [172] [On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05433)
*Hejian Sang,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang,Jiachen Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPSDC是一种通过自我蒸馏让推理模型变得更简洁的方法，仅使用"要简洁"的指令就能实现57-59%的token压缩，同时提升数学推理准确率9-16个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型虽然会"思考出声"，但大部分输出是噪声甚至有害的，冗余token会累积错误。现有方法需要真实答案、token预算或难度估计器，复杂度高。

Method: OPSDC基于自我蒸馏：让同一模型在"要简洁"指令下生成教师logits，然后在学生自身生成的推理轨迹上最小化每个token的反向KL散度。无需真实答案、token预算或难度估计器。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B上，MATH-500数据集实现57-59%的token压缩，准确率提升9-16个百分点；AIME 2024上14B模型准确率提升10个百分点，压缩41%。

Conclusion: OPSDC的简单性掩盖了其精妙性：能自动对简单问题进行激进压缩，同时保留困难问题所需的深思熟虑。推理模型的大部分输出不仅冗余，而且有害，每个不必要的token都会累积错误。

Abstract: Reasoning models think out loud, but much of what they say is noise. We introduce OPSDC (On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression), a method that teaches models to reason more concisely by
  distilling their own concise behavior back into themselves. The entire approach reduces to one idea: condition the same model on a "be concise" instruction to obtain teacher logits, and minimize per-token
  reverse KL on the student's own rollouts. No ground-truth answers, no token budgets, no difficulty estimators. Just self-distillation. Yet this simplicity belies surprising sophistication: OPSDC automatically
  compresses easy problems aggressively while preserving the deliberation needed for hard ones. On Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, we achieve 57-59% token reduction on MATH-500 while improving accuracy by 9-16 points
  absolute. On AIME 2024, the 14B model gains 10 points with 41% compression. The secret? Much of what reasoning models produce is not just redundant-it is actively harmful, compounding errors with every
  unnecessary token.

</details>


### [173] [Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05440)
*Siqi Yang,Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LWAIL框架，通过动态感知的潜在空间进行状态分布匹配，仅需少量无动作专家轨迹即可达到专家级性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习方法需要大量中等至高质量演示以及专家动作信息，这些在实际应用中往往难以获取。需要开发能够减少这些依赖的新方法。

Method: 提出LWAIL框架，核心是：1）在动态感知潜在空间中进行Wasserstein距离计算；2）通过预训练阶段训练ICVF来获取动态感知的潜在空间表示；3）仅使用状态信息进行分布匹配，无需专家动作。

Result: 在多个MuJoCo环境中，LWAIL超越了先前基于Wasserstein的模仿学习方法和其他对抗性模仿学习方法，仅需1个或少量状态专家轨迹即可达到专家级性能。

Conclusion: LWAIL通过动态感知潜在空间的状态分布匹配，显著减少了对专家演示的数量和质量要求，为数据稀缺场景下的模仿学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation Learning (IL) enables agents to mimic expert behavior by learning from demonstrations. However, traditional IL methods require large amounts of medium-to-high-quality demonstrations as well as actions of expert demonstrations, both of which are often unavailable. To reduce this need, we propose Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL), a novel adversarial imitation learning framework that focuses on state-only distribution matching. It benefits from the Wasserstein distance computed in a dynamics-aware latent space. This dynamics-aware latent space differs from prior work and is obtained via a pre-training stage, where we train the Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF) to capture a dynamics-aware structure of the state space using a small set of randomly generated state-only data. We show that this enhances the policy's understanding of state transitions, enabling the learning process to use only one or a few state-only expert episodes to achieve expert-level performance. Through experiments on multiple MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms prior Wasserstein-based IL methods and prior adversarial IL methods, achieving better results across various tasks.

</details>


### [174] [SurvHTE-Bench: A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation in Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05483)
*Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Xiaobin Shen,Jeremy C. Weiss,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurvHTE-Bench是首个针对右删失生存数据的异质性治疗效果估计的综合性基准测试，包含合成、半合成和真实世界数据集，旨在提供公平、可复现和可扩展的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析中的异质性治疗效果估计在精准医学和个体化政策制定等高风险应用中至关重要，但由于删失、未观测到的反事实和复杂的识别假设，该领域面临独特挑战。现有评估方法分散且不一致，缺乏统一的基准测试。

Method: 开发了SurvHTE-Bench基准测试，包含三个模块：(1) 具有已知真实值的模块化合成数据集套件，系统性地改变因果假设和生存动态；(2) 结合真实世界协变量与模拟治疗和结果的半合成数据集；(3) 来自双胞胎研究（已知真实值）和HIV临床试验的真实世界数据集。

Result: 该基准测试首次在合成、半合成和真实世界设置下，对不同条件和现实假设违反情况下的生存HTE方法进行了严格比较，为因果生存方法的评估奠定了基础。

Conclusion: SurvHTE-Bench为因果生存方法的公平、可复现和可扩展评估建立了基础，填补了该领域缺乏统一基准测试的空白，有助于推动异质性治疗效果估计方法的发展。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects (HTEs) from right-censored survival data is critical in high-stakes applications such as precision medicine and individualized policy-making. Yet, the survival analysis setting poses unique challenges for HTE estimation due to censoring, unobserved counterfactuals, and complex identification assumptions. Despite recent advances, from Causal Survival Forests to survival meta-learners and outcome imputation approaches, evaluation practices remain fragmented and inconsistent. We introduce SurvHTE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for HTE estimation with censored outcomes. The benchmark spans (i) a modular suite of synthetic datasets with known ground truth, systematically varying causal assumptions and survival dynamics, (ii) semi-synthetic datasets that pair real-world covariates with simulated treatments and outcomes, and (iii) real-world datasets from a twin study (with known ground truth) and from an HIV clinical trial. Across synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world settings, we provide the first rigorous comparison of survival HTE methods under diverse conditions and realistic assumption violations. SurvHTE-Bench establishes a foundation for fair, reproducible, and extensible evaluation of causal survival methods. The data and code of our benchmark are available at: https://github.com/Shahriarnz14/SurvHTE-Bench .

</details>


### [175] [Censored LLMs as a Natural Testbed for Secret Knowledge Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05494)
*Helena Casademunt,Bartosz Cywiński,Khoi Tran,Arya Jakkli,Samuel Marks,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估针对中文开源大语言模型在政治敏感话题上产生虚假回答的诚实性引导和谎言检测方法，发现没有技术能完全消除虚假回答


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要评估在专门训练说谎的模型上的诚实性引导和谎言检测方法，但这些人工构造可能不反映自然发生的虚假行为。本研究使用中国开发者开发的开源大语言模型作为测试平台，这些模型在政治敏感话题上经常产生虚假回答，偶尔会正确回答，表明它们拥有被训练压制但实际掌握的知识。

Method: 使用中国开发者开发的开源大语言模型（如Qwen3模型）作为测试平台，这些模型被训练审查政治敏感话题。评估一系列诚实性引导和谎言检测技术：对于诚实性引导，测试了不使用聊天模板的采样、少样本提示以及在通用诚实数据上的微调；对于谎言检测，测试了让审查模型对自己的回答进行分类，以及在不相关数据上训练的线性探针。

Result: 诚实性引导方面：不使用聊天模板的采样、少样本提示以及在通用诚实数据上的微调最可靠地增加了真实回答。谎言检测方面：让审查模型对自己的回答进行分类的表现接近未审查模型的上限，而在不相关数据上训练的线性探针提供了更便宜的替代方案。最强的诚实性引导技术也能迁移到前沿开源模型如DeepSeek R1。值得注意的是，没有任何技术能完全消除虚假回答。

Conclusion: 研究提供了在自然发生的虚假行为场景下评估诚实性引导和谎言检测方法的测试平台。虽然某些技术能提高真实回答率，但没有方法能完全消除虚假回答。研究强调了在真实世界场景中评估这些方法的重要性，并发布了所有提示、代码和转录文本供进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models sometimes produce false or misleading responses. Two approaches to this problem are honesty elicitation -- modifying prompts or weights so that the model answers truthfully -- and lie detection -- classifying whether a given response is false. Prior work evaluates such methods on models specifically trained to lie or conceal information, but these artificial constructions may not resemble naturally-occurring dishonesty. We instead study open-weights LLMs from Chinese developers, which are trained to censor politically sensitive topics: Qwen3 models frequently produce falsehoods about subjects like Falun Gong or the Tiananmen protests while occasionally answering correctly, indicating they possess knowledge they are trained to suppress. Using this as a testbed, we evaluate a suite of elicitation and lie detection techniques. For honesty elicitation, sampling without a chat template, few-shot prompting, and fine-tuning on generic honesty data most reliably increase truthful responses. For lie detection, prompting the censored model to classify its own responses performs near an uncensored-model upper bound, and linear probes trained on unrelated data offer a cheaper alternative. The strongest honesty elicitation techniques also transfer to frontier open-weights models including DeepSeek R1. Notably, no technique fully eliminates false responses. We release all prompts, code, and transcripts.

</details>


### [176] [POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05500)
*Zeju Qiu,Lixin Liu,Adrian Weller,Han Shi,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POET-X：一种可扩展且内存高效的变体，通过正交等价变换优化大语言模型训练，在保持POET泛化性和稳定性优势的同时，显著降低计算成本和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然POET（重参数化正交等价训练）框架为大语言模型提供了强大的训练稳定性，但其原始实现由于密集的矩阵乘法导致高内存消耗和计算开销，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出POET-X，一种可扩展且内存高效的变体，通过改进的正交等价变换方法，显著降低计算成本，同时保持谱保持特性。

Result: POET-X能够在单个Nvidia H100 GPU上预训练十亿参数级别的大语言模型，而标准优化器如AdamW在相同设置下会内存不足；同时保持了POET的泛化性和稳定性优势，并实现了吞吐量和内存效率的显著提升。

Conclusion: POET-X通过高效的正交等价变换，解决了大语言模型训练中的内存和计算瓶颈问题，为在有限硬件资源下训练大规模模型提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient and stable training of large language models (LLMs) remains a core challenge in modern machine learning systems. To address this challenge, Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training (POET), a spectrum-preserving framework that optimizes each weight matrix through orthogonal equivalence transformation, has been proposed. Although POET provides strong training stability, its original implementation incurs high memory consumption and computational overhead due to intensive matrix multiplications. To overcome these limitations, we introduce POET-X, a scalable and memory-efficient variant that performs orthogonal equivalence transformations with significantly reduced computational cost. POET-X maintains the generalization and stability benefits of POET while achieving substantial improvements in throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, POET-X enables the pretraining of billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, and in contrast, standard optimizers such as AdamW run out of memory under the same settings.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Multistage Stochastic Programming for Rare Event Risk Mitigation in Power Systems Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04734)
*Daniel Mastropietro,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多阶段场景优化的罕见事件感知电力系统控制方法，使用Fleming-Viot粒子方法偏置场景生成，以应对长时间可再生能源短缺的极端情况


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高比例间歇性可再生能源渗透给电力系统运行管理带来鲁棒性挑战。当天气分布的尾部实现导致长时间太阳能辐照和风速不足时，需要提前启动传统发电机组，但预测不准确会导致浪费或灾难性供电不足，因此需要准确建模噪声和动态过程

Method: 采用多阶段场景优化方法进行罕见事件感知控制，使用Fleming-Viot粒子方法偏置场景生成，特别关注极低风电功率的罕见实现，以获得成本效益高的传统发电机组控制策略

Result: 该方法能够生成针对罕见可再生能源短缺事件的鲁棒控制策略，在长时间可再生能源短缺情况下实现成本有效的传统发电机组调度

Conclusion: 提出的罕见事件感知控制方法能够提高高比例可再生能源电力系统在极端天气条件下的运行鲁棒性，通过偏置场景生成优化传统发电机组的调度决策

Abstract: High intermittent renewable penetration in the energy mix presents challenges in robustness for the management of power systems' operation. If a tail realization of the distribution of weather yields a prolonged period of time during which solar irradiation and wind speed are insufficient for satisfying energy demand, then it becomes critical to ramp up the generation of conventional power plants with adequate foresight. This event trigger is costly, and inaccurate forecasting can either be wasteful or yield catastrophic undersupply. This encourages particular attention to accurate modeling of the noise and the resulting dynamics within the aforementioned scenario. In this work we present a method for rare event-aware control of power systems using multi-stage scenario-based optimization. A Fleming-Viot particle approach is used to bias the scenario generation towards rare realizations of very low wind power, in order to obtain a cost-effective control of conventional power plants that is robust under prolonged renewable energy shortfalls.

</details>


### [178] [Policy Optimization of Mixed H2/H-infinity Control: Benign Nonconvexity and Global Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04843)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yuto Watanabe,Yujie Tang,Yang Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文从现代策略优化视角重新审视混合H2/H∞控制，揭示了其良性的非凸结构：所有驻点都是全局最优解，并提出了扩展凸提升框架来连接非凸策略优化与凸重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Riccati/LMI方法对混合H2/H∞控制的非凸优化景观提供有限洞察，且难以扩展到大规模或数据驱动场景。需要从现代策略优化视角重新审视该问题，以揭示其内在结构并设计可扩展算法。

Method: 采用策略优化视角分析混合H2/H∞控制，包括通用双通道和单通道情况。提出扩展凸提升（ECL）框架，通过非严格Riccati不等式连接非凸策略优化与凸重构，并推导出显式梯度公式。

Result: 证明了混合H2/H∞控制具有良性非凸结构：所有驻点都是全局最优解。刻画了H∞约束可行集（开集、路径连通，边界由饱和H∞约束的策略给出）。混合目标函数在内部是实解析的，并给出了显式梯度公式。

Conclusion: 混合H2/H∞控制存在隐藏的凸性结构，扩展凸提升框架揭示了这一特性，为设计可扩展的大规模策略迭代方法提供了理论基础，使混合控制问题更适用于现代大规模和数据驱动场景。

Abstract: Mixed H2/H-infinity control balances performance and robustness by minimizing an H2 cost bound subject to an H-infinity constraint. However, classical Riccati/LMI solutions offer limited insight into the nonconvex optimization landscape and do not readily scale to large-scale or data-driven settings. In this paper, we revisit mixed H2/H-infinity control from a modern policy optimization viewpoint, including the general two-channel and single-channel cases. One central result is that both cases enjoy a benign nonconvex structure: every stationary point is globally optimal. We characterize the H-infinity-constrained feasible set, which is open, path-connected, with boundary given exactly by policies saturating the H-infinity constraint. We also show that the mixed objective is real analytic in the interior with explicit gradient formulas. Our key analysis builds on an Extended Convex Lifting (ECL) framework that bridges nonconvex policy optimization and convex reformulations. The ECL constructions rely on non-strict Riccati inequalities that allow us to characterize global optimality. These insights reveal hidden convexity in mixed H2/H-infinity control and facilitate the design of scalable policy iteration methods in large-scale settings.

</details>


### [179] [A class of stochastic control problems with state constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04880)
*Tiziano De Angelis,Erik Ekström*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种解决带状态约束的线性二次最优控制问题的概率方法，通过概率表示获得价值函数和最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有状态约束的线性二次最优控制问题，这类问题在金融、工程等领域有广泛应用，但传统方法在处理状态约束时存在困难。

Method: 使用概率方法，将最优控制问题转化为概率表示问题。在扩散过程X必须保持在集合C内的约束下，通过线性控制最小化二次成本。在底层动态系数和集合D的正则性满足温和充分条件下，获得价值函数的概率表示和最优控制。

Result: 获得了价值函数的概率表示和最优控制，最优控制动态以强形式存在（适应于驱动布朗运动的滤波）。在一些相关示例中给出了完全显式公式。

Conclusion: 该研究为带状态约束的线性二次最优控制问题提供了一种有效的概率解决方案，在温和条件下获得了理论结果和显式公式，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We obtain a probabilistic solution to linear-quadratic optimal control problems with state constraints. Given a closed set $\mathcal{D}\subseteq [0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^d$, a diffusion $X$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$ must be linearly controlled in order to keep the time-space process $(t,X_t)$ inside the set $\mathcal{C}:=([0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^d)\setminus\mathcal{D}$, while at the same time minimising an expected cost that depends on the state $(t,X_t)$ and is quadratic in the speed of the control exerted. We find a probabilistic representation for the value function and an optimal control under a set of mild sufficient conditions concerning the coefficients of the underlying dynamics and the regularity of the set $\mathcal{D}$. The optimally controlled dynamics is in strong form, in the sense that it is adapted to the filtration generated by the driving Brownian motion. Fully explicit formulae are presented in some relevant examples.

</details>


### [180] [Biquadratic SOS Rank and Double Zarankiewicz Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04912)
*Liqun Qi,Chunfeng Cui,Yi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文引入了双Zarankiewicz数z₂(m,n)来刻画双二次形式的最大SOS秩，通过允许普通边和"双边"的二分图构造，改进了BSR(m,n)的下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现双二次形式的最大SOS秩BSR(m,n)下界由经典Zarankiewicz数z(m,n)给出，但在4×3情况下存在间隙：BSR(4,3)≥8 > z(4,3)=7。这表明需要新的理论框架来捕捉由双线性形式平方引起的额外结构。

Method: 引入双Zarankiewicz数z₂(m,n)，定义包含普通边(1-边)和"双边"(2-边)的新二分图类，后者对应简单两项双线性形式的平方。证明如果图G不含广义C₄-环，则sos(P_G)=|E₁|+|E₂|，从而BSR(m,n)≥z₂(m,n)。计算了小参数下的精确值。

Result: 确定了多个小参数下的精确值：z₂(m,2)=m+1，z₂(2,n)=n+1，z₂(3,3)=6，z₂(4,3)=8，z₂(5,3)=9。对于4×4情况，证明10≤z₂(4,4)≤11。这些结果改进了BSR(4,4)和BSR(5,3)的已知下界。

Conclusion: 双Zarankiewicz数z₂(m,n)为理解双二次形式的SOS秩提供了更精确的理论框架，揭示了极值图论与SOS表示研究之间的深刻联系。4×4情况的精确值仍为开放问题，为进一步研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The maximum sum-of-squares (SOS) rank of an $m\times n$ biquadratic form, denoted $\operatorname{BSR}(m,n)$, has recently been shown to be bounded below by the classical Zarankiewicz number $z(m,n)$, the maximum number of edges in a $C_4$-free bipartite graph. However, a gap was discovered for the $4\times3$ case: $\operatorname{BSR}(4,3)\ge 8 > z(4,3)=7$, arising from a construction that adds a square of a bilinear form $(x_4y_2+x_1y_3)^2$ to a $C_4$-free simple biquadratic form. This paper introduces the double Zarankiewicz number $z_2(m,n)$ to capture such phenomena. We define a new class of bipartite graphs that may contain both ordinary edges (1-edges) and ``double edges'' (2-edges), the latter corresponding to squares of simple two-term bilinear forms. A graph $G$ of this type yields a doubly simple biquadratic form $P_G$, and we prove that if $G$ contains no \emph{generalised $C_4$-cycle} then $\operatorname{sos}(P_G)=|E_1|+|E_2|$; consequently $\operatorname{BSR}(m,n)\ge z_2(m,n)$. We determine the exact value of $z_2(m,n)$ for several small parameters: $z_2(m,2)=m+1$, $z_2(2,n)=n+1$, $z_2(3,3)=6$, $z_2(4,3)=8$, and $z_2(5,3)=9$. For the $4\times4$ case we establish $10\le z_2(4,4)\le 11$, leaving the exact value as an open problem. These results improve the known lower bounds for $\operatorname{BSR}(4,4)$ and $\operatorname{BSR}(5,3)$. The paper concludes with a discussion of open questions and connections between extremal graph theory and the study of SOS representations.

</details>


### [181] [An Efficient Stochastic First-Order Algorithm for Nonconvex-Strongly Concave Minimax Optimization beyond Lipschitz Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04940)
*Yan Gao,Yongchao Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出NSGDA-M算法解决广义光滑条件下的非凸随机极小极大问题，在非凸-强凹目标函数下达到O(ε⁻⁴)的梯度复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸随机极小极大算法大多基于标准Lipschitz光滑假设，本文研究广义光滑条件下的随机极小极大问题，旨在开发更通用的算法框架

Method: 提出NSGDA-M算法：内层变量使用随机梯度上升更新，外层变量使用带动量的归一化随机梯度下降更新，适用于广义光滑条件

Result: 对于非凸-强凹目标函数，NSGDA-M在期望意义下需要O(ε⁻⁴)次随机梯度评估找到ε-稳定点，高概率意义下需要O(ε⁻⁴(log(1/δ))³/²)次评估

Conclusion: NSGDA-M算法在广义光滑条件下有效解决非凸随机极小极大问题，理论分析和分布鲁棒优化实验验证了其有效性

Abstract: In recent years, nonconvex minimax problems have attracted significant attention due to their broad applications in machine learning, including generative adversarial networks, robust optimization and adversarial training. Most existing algorithms for nonconvex stochastic minimax problems are developed under the standard Lipschitz smoothness assumption. In this paper, we study stochastic minimax problems under a generalized smoothness condition and propose an algorithm, NSGDA-M, which simultaneously updates the inner variable by stochastic gradient ascent and updates the outer variable by normalized stochastic gradient descent with momentum. When the objective function is nonconvex-strongly concave, we show that NSGDA-M finds an $ε$-stationary point of the primal function within $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ stochastic gradient evaluations in expectation, and $\mathcal{O}\left(ε^{-4}(\log(\frac{1}δ))^{3/2}\right)$ stochastic gradient evaluations in high probability, where $δ\in (0,1)$ is the failure probability. We verify the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm through numerical experiments on a distributionally robust optimization problem.

</details>


### [182] [Stochastic Optimal Feedforward-Feedback Control for Partially Observable Sensorimotor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04994)
*Bastien Berret,Frédéric Jean*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个连续时间框架，通过扩展邻近最优控制，使前馈计划能显式考虑反馈不确定性和延迟，用于部分可观测、随机、非线性、高维系统的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工程和生物系统的鲁棒控制依赖于前馈和反馈机制的整合。神经运动控制中，前馈肌肉协同收缩与感觉驱动的反馈校正共同确保稳定行为。然而，为部分可观测、随机、非线性、高维系统推导通用的连续时间框架来确定最优控制策略仍是一个巨大的计算挑战。

Method: 扩展邻近最优控制，使前馈计划能显式考虑反馈不确定性和延迟。使用统计线性化将随机问题转化为近似等价的确定性优化，在可处理的增强状态空间中保留关键非线性，提供机制可解释性和近似保真度的理论保证。

Result: 将该框架应用于人类神经力学，证明肌肉协同收缩是适应任务需求的最优策略，考虑到感觉运动系统的特性。结果提供了神经运动控制的计算基础和非线性随机系统控制的通用工具。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为部分可观测、随机、非线性、高维系统的控制提供了通用解决方案，特别适用于神经运动控制，并具有机制可解释性和理论保证。

Abstract: Robust control of complex engineered and biological systems hinges on the integration of feedforward and feedback mechanisms. This is exemplified in neural motor control, where feedforward muscle co-contraction complements sensory-driven feedback corrections to ensure stable behaviors. However, deriving a general continuous-time framework to determine such optimal control policies for partially observable, stochastic, nonlinear, and high-dimensional systems remains a formidable computational challenge. Here, we introduce a framework that extends neighboring optimal control by enabling the feedforward plan to explicitly account for feedback uncertainties and latencies. Using statistical linearization, we transform the stochastic problem into an approximately equivalent deterministic optimization within a tractable, augmented state space that retains critical nonlinearities, offering both mechanistic interpretability and theoretical guarantees on approximation fidelity. We apply this framework to human neuromechanics, demonstrating that muscle co-contraction emerges as an optimal adaptation to task demands, given the characteristics of our sensorimotor system. Our results provide a computational foundation for neuromotor control and a generalizable tool for the control of nonlinear stochastic systems.

</details>


### [183] [A Second-Order Algorithm Based on Affine Scaling Interior-Point Methods for nonlinear minimization with bound constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05022)
*Yonggang Pei,Yubing Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将HSODM扩展到带边界约束的非线性优化问题，提出基于仿射尺度内点法的二阶算法SOBASIP，实现O(ε^(-3/2))全局复杂度和局部超线性收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSODM在无约束优化中表现出色，但需要扩展到带边界约束的问题。现有二阶方法在处理边界约束时存在局限性，需要开发既能保持HSODM优势又能处理约束的高效算法

Method: 提出SOBASIP算法：1) 引入适当仿射矩阵构造仿射尺度子问题；2) 使用齐次化技术将子问题转化为普通齐次模型(OHM)，本质为特征值问题；3) 从OHM最优解构造下降方向；4) 使用回溯线搜索确定新迭代点

Result: 理论分析表明：SOBASIP达到O(ε^(-3/2))全局迭代复杂度找到ε-近似二阶稳定点；在特定条件下实现局部超线性收敛；数值实验显示算法性能令人满意

Conclusion: 成功将HSODM扩展到边界约束问题，提出的SOBASIP算法在理论和数值上均表现良好，为带边界约束的二阶优化提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: The homogeneous second-order descent method (HSODM: Zhang et al. in Mathematics of Operations Research (2025)) was initially proposed for unconstrained optimization problems. HSODM shows excellent performance with respect to the global complexity rate among a certain broad class of second-order methods. In this paper, we extend HSODM to solve nonlinear optimization problems with bound constraints and propose a second-order algorithm based on affine scaling interior-point methods (SOBASIP). In each iteration, an appropriate affine matrix is introduced to construct an affine scaling subproblem based on the optimality conditions of the problem. To obtain a valid descent direction similar to HSODM, we utilize the homogenization technique to transform the scaling subproblem into an Ordinary Homogeneous Model (OHM), which is essentially an eigenvalue problem that can be solved efficiently. The descent direction is constructed from the optimal solution to the OHM, and then backtracking line search is used to determine the new iteration point. Theoretical analysis establishes that SOBASIP achieves a global iteration complexity of O(epsilon^(-3/2)) for finding an epsilon-approximate second-order stationary point and converges locally at a superlinear rate under certain conditions. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method exhibits satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [184] [Robust adaptive NMPC using ellipsoidal tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05029)
*Johannes Buerger,Mark Cannon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种计算高效的非线性模型预测控制算法，用于安全的学习型控制，采用椭球管方法处理模型不确定性和扰动，保证约束满足和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对学习型控制中的模型不确定性、扰动和计算效率问题，需要一种能够保证安全性和稳定性的非线性模型预测控制算法。

Method: 使用基函数仿射组合表示系统模型，采用连续线性化方法，利用椭球集合处理线性化误差、模型误差和扰动，结合集合成员参数估计和回溯线搜索。

Result: 椭球嵌入模型不确定性的方法在数值模拟中表现出良好的维度扩展性，算法能够保证递归可行性和输入到状态实际稳定性。

Conclusion: 该算法为学习型控制提供了一种计算高效且具有安全保证的非线性模型预测控制框架，能够有效处理模型不确定性和扰动。

Abstract: We propose a computationally efficient nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) algorithm for safe, learning-based control. The system model is represented as an affine combination of basis functions with unknown parameters, and is subject to additive set-bounded disturbances. Our algorithm employs successive linearization around nominal predicted trajectories and accounts for uncertainties in predicted states due to linearization, model errors, and disturbances using ellipsoidal sets. The ellipsoidal tube-based approach ensures that constraints on control inputs and system states are satisfied. Robustness to uncertainty is ensured using bounds on linearization errors and a backtracking line search. We show that the ellipsoidal embedding of model uncertainty scales favourably with system dimensions in numerical simulations. The algorithm incorporates set membership parameter estimation, and provides guarantees of recursive feasibility and input-to-state practical stability.

</details>


### [185] [Solution of a bilevel optimistic scheduling problem on parallel machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05107)
*Quentin Schau,Olivier Ploton,Vincent T'kindt,Han Hoogeveen,Federico Della Croce,Jippe Hoogeveen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了乐观双层优化框架下的均匀并行机调度问题，其中领导者最小化加权延迟作业数，追随者最小化总完工时间，证明了问题的强NP难性并提出了动态规划、MIP和分支定界算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于工业4.0背景下，需要解决具有双层决策结构的调度问题，其中领导者（如管理者）和追随者（如操作者）具有不同的优化目标，这种问题在实际生产调度中具有重要应用价值。

Method: 论文采用多种方法：1) 通过从Numerical 3-Dimensional Matching问题归约证明了问题的强NP难性；2) 提出了中等指数时间的动态规划算法；3) 建立了简洁的混合整数规划(MIP)公式；4) 设计了嵌入列生成方法计算下界的分支定界算法。

Result: 计算实验表明，所提出的方法能够处理最多80个作业和4台机器的实例，但对于更大规模的问题，现有方法仍难以有效求解，显示了问题的计算复杂性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功分析了乐观双层优化框架下的均匀并行机调度问题的计算复杂性，提出了多种求解方法，为工业4.0环境中的实际调度问题提供了理论框架和算法基础，但大规模实例的求解仍面临挑战。

Abstract: We consider the uniform parallel machines scheduling problem in the context of optimistic bilevel optimization, where two speed options are considered. In this scenario, the leader aims to minimize the weighted number of tardy jobs, while the follower seeks to minimize the total completion time on a set of uniform machines. This problem has practical applications in Industry 4.0. We show that this problem is NP-hard in the strong sense by providing a reduction from the Numerical 3-Dimensional Matching problem and we provide a moderately exponential-time dynamic programming algorithm. The problem is solved by means of a concise MIP formulation and a branch-and-bound algorithm that embeds a column generation approach for the lower bound computation. Computational experiments are presented for instances with up to 80 jobs and 4 machines while larger problems are out of reach for the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [186] [Optimization with Parametric Variational Inequality Constraints on a Moving Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05196)
*Xiaojun Chen,Jin Zhang,Yixuan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究由移动集上定义的参数变分不等式(PVI)约束的优化问题，提出SIGA算法并证明其收敛性，应用于投资组合管理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于均衡约束数学规划的研究大多只考虑函数中的参数，而本文关注约束集也随参数变化的参数变分不等式(PVI)，这种更一般的框架在投资组合管理等实际问题中具有重要应用价值。

Method: 提出平滑隐式梯度算法(SIGA)，基于PVI的平滑近似来求解优化问题。首先证明PVI解函数的Lipschitz连续性、优化问题解集非空有界性以及约束的度量正则性，然后利用这些理论性质设计算法。

Result: 理论证明：PVI解函数关于上层决策变量Lipschitz连续；优化问题解集非空有界；约束自动满足度量正则性；SIGA算法收敛到优化问题的稳定点。数值实验：在真实数据的投资组合管理问题上验证了SIGA算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为移动集上参数变分不等式约束的优化问题提供了完整的理论分析和有效的数值算法，SIGA算法在无需额外假设条件下能收敛到稳定点，并在实际投资组合问题中表现出良好性能。

Abstract: This paper focuses on optimization problems constrained by Parametric Variational Inequalities (PVI) defined on a moving set. Unlike most existing works on mathematical programs with equilibrium constraints, the equilibrium constraints have parameters not only in the function but also in the related set. We show that the solution function of the PVI is Lipschitz continuous with respect to the upper-level decision variables and the solution set of the optimization problem is nonempty and bounded. Moreover, we prove that the metric regularity of the constraints holds automatically, which allow us to characterize stationary points without any additional assumptions. A Smoothing Implicit Gradient Algorithm (SIGA) is proposed based on the smoothing approximation of the PVI. We prove the convergence of SIGA to a stationary point of the optimization problem and numerically validate the efficiency of SIGA by portfolio management problems with real data.

</details>


### [187] [Integral Formulation and the Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles-Type Principle for Prox-Regular Sweeping Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05376)
*Juan Guillermo Garrido,Emilio Vilches*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了希尔伯特空间中由时间依赖的一致邻近正则集驱动的扫掠过程，允许移动约束具有有界变差间断性。作者提出了一种新的积分公式，并将其与标准的微分测度公式等价性证明，为邻近正则扫掠过程提供了统一的有界变差解概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究邻近正则（通常非凸）框架下的扫掠过程，处理移动约束具有有界变差间断性的情况。现有微分测度公式在非凸情况下存在局限性，需要发展新的积分公式来统一有界变差解的概念。

Method: 引入新的积分公式：基于全局变分不等式，测试于连续容许轨迹，包含反映邻近法锥次单调性的二次修正项。在移动集满足时间下半连续性、一致邻近正则性和选择-扩展性质等温和正则性假设下，证明该积分公式与标准微分测度公式等价。

Result: 证明了新积分公式与微分测度公式的等价性，建立了统一的邻近正则扫掠过程有界变差解概念。进一步获得了Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles型变分特征：通过邻近正则变分残差，解是使残差达到最大值（零）的轨迹。建立了稳定性结果：具有消失残差的容许轨迹的一致极限是极限扫掠过程的解。

Conclusion: 该研究为邻近正则非凸框架下的扫掠过程提供了新的积分公式和变分特征，建立了统一的解概念和稳定性理论。所得变分框架为邻近正则非凸设置中的稳定性和近似分析提供了鲁棒工具。

Abstract: We study sweeping processes in a Hilbert space driven by time-dependent uniformly prox-regular sets, allowing the moving constraint to exhibit discontinuities of bounded variation. We introduce a new integral formulation for bounded-variation trajectories, given by a global variational inequality tested against continuous admissible trajectories, and we compare it with the standard differential-measure formulation, in which the differential measure of the trajectory is constrained by the proximal normal cone. In the prox-regular (generally nonconvex) framework, the variational inequality necessarily includes a quadratic correction term reflecting the hypomonotonicity of proximal normal cones.
  Under mild regularity assumptions on the moving set, including lower semicontinuity in time, uniform prox-regularity of the values, and a selection-extension property guaranteeing a rich class of test trajectories (satisfied, for instance, in the convex case and for bounded prox-regular sets), we prove that the new integral formulation is equivalent to the differential-measure formulation. This yields a unified bounded-variation notion of solution for prox-regular sweeping processes.
  We further establish a Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles-type variational characterization via a prox-regular variational residual: the residual is nonpositive along every admissible trajectory, and solutions are exactly those trajectories for which this residual attains its maximal value, namely zero. As a consequence, we prove a stability result: a uniform limit of admissible trajectories with vanishing residual is a solution of the limit sweeping process. The resulting variational framework provides a robust tool for stability and approximation analyses in the prox-regular, nonconvex setting.

</details>


### [188] [The Inverse Micromechanics Problem given Dielectric Constants for Isotropic Composites with Spherical Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05460)
*Athindra Pavan,Swaroop Darbha,Bjorn Birgisson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将凸优化引入Eshelby基逆向微力学问题，聚焦于使用Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka模型根据复合材料及其各组分的介电常数确定微观结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正向微力学问题关注给定组分性质和微观结构信息时确定复合材料有效性能，而逆向微力学问题则相反：给定复合材料及其所有组分的性能，确定微观结构信息。该研究旨在开发一种系统方法来解决这一逆向问题。

Method: 采用Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka模型处理各向同性复合材料中的球形夹杂物，将逆向微力学问题表述为线性规划问题，并通过凸优化方法求解。模型选择基于其相对简单且对球状夹杂物具有闭合形式解析解的特点。

Result: 成功将逆向微力学问题转化为线性规划问题并求解，展示了弥散材料、测量噪声与所得体积分数分割质量之间的关系，验证了凸优化在逆向微力学中的适用性。

Conclusion: 凸优化是研究Eshelby基逆向微力学问题的有效工具，能够系统解决给定材料性能时确定微观结构信息的问题，为复合材料设计提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: In this article, convex optimization is introduced as a promising tool to study Eshelby based inverse micromechanics problems. The focus is on inverse micromechanics using the Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka model given the dielectric constants of the composite material and of all of its components. The model is exactly the same for the conductivity properties as well. This choice of model is made since the model is fairly simple and has a closed form analytical solution for the case of spheroidal inclusions as well. The forward or direct micromechanics problem deals with the determination of effective properties of a composite material given the properties of its components and microstructural information. The focus is on isotropic composites and the distribution of inclusions is assumed to be such that this holds. The inverse micromechanics problem considered in this paper deals with the determination of microstructural information given the properties of the composite material and all of its components. Since in this paper, isotropy of the composite and only spherical inclusions are considered, the goal is to determine just volume fractions of the components of the composite material. The inverse problem is formulated as a Linear Programming problem and is solved. Before this, the inverse problem and certain important variants of it are examined through the lens of convex optimization. Lastly, promising results regarding the relationship between dispersive materials, noise in measurements, and quality of obtained volumetric splits are showcased. The scope of the use of convex optimization in inverse micromechanics is discussed.

</details>
